Division : Medium Project Divisional Team, Ambadpal, Taluka-Kudal, Dist. Sindhudurg

MMISF ACT 2005
( SEE SECTION 5.0,6.0,7.7 AND RULE 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User Associations (WUA’s)
on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF
Act 2005, Govind Shrimangle , Executive Engineer, Minor Irrigation Division No.1,Sindhudurg hereby
delineate areas of Operation of following WUA’s Direct that the certified copy of the updated map
and list of land holders and/or occupiers of said WUA’s shall be displayed on the notice board of the
offices of concerned Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub-Division and Division
and other prominent public places.
I, Govind Shrimangle, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be supplied
by the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land and the system of supply of
water through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands
under Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days from the date
of publication in the official Gazette file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Sindhudurg
Irrigation Circle, Sindhudurg within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the
Government Gazette.

Division Medium Project Divisional Unit, Ambadpal, Taluka-Kudal, Dist. Sindhudurg
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User Associations (WUA's)
on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF
Act 2005,I Govind Shrimangle , Executive Engineer, Medium Project Divisional Unit,Sindhudurg hereby
delineate areas of Operation of following WUA's Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and
list of land holders and/or occupiers of said WUA's shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices
of concerned Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, Govind Shrimangle, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be supplied by
the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of water
through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days from the date of
publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Sindhudurg Irrigation
Circle, Sindhudurg within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government
Gazette.
(Rupesh Chavan) (Govind Shrimangle)
Sub Divisional Engineer Executive Engineer
Medium Project Divisional Unit Medium Project Divisional Unit
Kudal-Ambadpal Kudal-Ambadpal

Division : Medium Project Divisional Unit, Ambadpal, Taluka-Kudal, Dist. Sindhudurg
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User Associations (WUA's) on
hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005,I
Govind Shrimangle , Executive Engineer, Medium Project Divisional Unit,Sindhudurg hereby delineate areas of
Operation of following WUA's Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders and/or
occupiers of said WUA's shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned Grampanchayat, Tahasil
Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other prominent public places.
I, Govind Shrimangle, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be supplied by the
appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of water through Water User
Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system
by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days from the date of publication in the
official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Sindhudurg Irrigation Circle, Sindhudurg within 30
days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.

Schedule –‘A’

Division Medium Project Divisional Unit, Ambadpal, Taluka-Kudal, Dist. Sindhudurg
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User Associations (WUA's)
on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF
Act 2005,I Govind Shrimangle , Executive Engineer, Medium Project Divisional Unit,Sindhudurg hereby
delineate areas of Operation of following WUA's Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and
list of land holders and/or occupiers of said WUA's shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices
of concerned Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, Govind Shrimangle, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be supplied by
the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of water
through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days from the date of
publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Sindhudurg Irrigation
Circle, Sindhudurg within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government
Gazette

Division Medium Project Divisional Unit, Ambadpal, Taluka-Kudal, Dist. Sindhudurg
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User Associations (WUA's)
on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF
Act 2005,I Govind Shrimangle , Executive Engineer, Medium Project Divisional Unit,Sindhudurg hereby
delineate areas of Operation of following WUA's Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and
list of land holders and/or occupiers of said WUA's shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices
of concerned Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, Govind Shrimangle, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be supplied by
the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of water
through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days from the date of
publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Sindhudurg Irrigation
Circle, Sindhudurg within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government
Gazette.

Schedule –‘A’

Division : Medium Project Divisional Unit, Ambadpal, Taluka-Kudal, Dist. Sindhudurg
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User Associations (WUA's) on
hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF Act 2005,I
Govind Shrimangle , Executive Engineer, Medium Project Divisional Unit,Sindhudurg hereby delineate areas of
Operation of following WUA's Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and list of land holders and/or
occupiers of said WUA's shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices of concerned Grampanchayat, Tahasil
Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other prominent public places.
I, Govind Shrimangle, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be supplied by the
appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of water through Water User
Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under Management of Irrigation system
by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days from the date of publication in the
official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Sindhudurg Irrigation Circle, Sindhudurg within 30
days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government Gazette.

Division Medium Project Divisional Unit, Ambadpal, Taluka-Kudal, Dist. Sindhudurg
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User Associations (WUA's)
on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF
Act 2005,I Govind Shrimangle , Executive Engineer, Medium Project Divisional Unit,Sindhudurg hereby
delineate areas of Operation of following WUA's Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and
list of land holders and/or occupiers of said WUA's shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices
of concerned Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, Govind Shrimangle, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be supplied by
the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of water
through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days from the date of
publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Sindhudurg Irrigation
Circle, Sindhudurg within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government
Gazette

Division Medium Project Divisional Unit, Ambadpal, Taluka-Kudal, Dist. Sindhudurg
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User Associations (WUA's)
on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF
Act 2005,I Govind Shrimangle , Executive Engineer, Medium Project Divisional Unit,Sindhudurg hereby
delineate areas of Operation of following WUA's Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and
list of land holders and/or occupiers of said WUA's shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices
of concerned Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, Govind Shrimangle, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be supplied by
the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of water
through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days from the date of
publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Sindhudurg Irrigation
Circle, Sindhudurg within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government
Gazette

Division Medium Project Divisional Unit, Ambadpal, Taluka-Kudal, Dist. Sindhudurg
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User Associations (WUA's)
on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF
Act 2005,I Govind Shrimangle , Executive Engineer, Medium Project Divisional Unit,Sindhudurg hereby
delineate areas of Operation of following WUA's Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and
list of land holders and/or occupiers of said WUA's shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices
of concerned Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, Govind Shrimangle, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be supplied by
the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of water
through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days from the date of
publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Sindhudurg Irrigation
Circle, Sindhudurg within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government
Gazette

Division Medium Project Divisional Unit, Ambadpal, Taluka-Kudal, Dist. Sindhudurg
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User Associations (WUA's)
on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF
Act 2005,I Govind Shrimangle , Executive Engineer, Medium Project Divisional Unit,Sindhudurg hereby
delineate areas of Operation of following WUA's Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and
list of land holders and/or occupiers of said WUA's shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices
of concerned Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, Govind Shrimangle, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be supplied by
the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of water
through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days from the date of
publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Sindhudurg Irrigation
Circle, Sindhudurg within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government
Gazette

Schedule –‘A’

Division Medium Project Divisional Unit, Ambadpal, Taluka-Kudal, Dist. Sindhudurg
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User Associations (WUA's)
on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF
Act 2005,I Govind Shrimangle , Executive Engineer, Medium Project Divisional Unit,Sindhudurg hereby
delineate areas of Operation of following WUA's Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and
list of land holders and/or occupiers of said WUA's shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices
of concerned Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, Govind Shrimangle, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be supplied by
the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of water
through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days from the date of
publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Sindhudurg Irrigation
Circle, Sindhudurg within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government
Gazette

Schedule –‘A’

Division Medium Project Divisional Unit, Ambadpal, Taluka-Kudal, Dist. Sindhudurg
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User Associations (WUA's)
on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF
Act 2005,I Govind Shrimangle , Executive Engineer, Medium Project Divisional Unit,Sindhudurg hereby
delineate areas of Operation of following WUA's Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and
list of land holders and/or occupiers of said WUA's shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices
of concerned Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, Govind Shrimangle, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be supplied by
the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of water
through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days from the date of
publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Sindhudurg Irrigation
Circle, Sindhudurg within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government
Gazette

Schedule –‘A’

Division Medium Project Divisional Unit, Ambadpal, Taluka-Kudal, Dist. Sindhudurg
MMISF ACT 2005
( See section 5.0,6.0,7.7 and Rule 3.00 )
NOTIFICATION NO – III
Where it has been decided to delinate the Area of Operation of Water User Associations (WUA's)
on hydraulic basis and as per administrative convenience under section 5,6,7 and Rule 3 of the MMISF
Act 2005,I Govind Shrimangle , Executive Engineer, Medium Project Divisional Unit,Sindhudurg hereby
delineate areas of Operation of following WUA's Direct that the certified copy of the updated map and
list of land holders and/or occupiers of said WUA's shall be displayed on the notice board of the offices
of concerned Grampanchayat, Tahasil Office, Irrigation Section, Sub Division and Division and other
prominent public places.
I, Govind Shrimangle, Executive Engineer, also hereby declare that no water shall be supplied by
the appropriate authority to an individual or occupier of such land & the system of supply of water
through Water User Association shall binding on all the holders and the occupiers of the lands under
Management of Irrigation system by farmers.
Any person affected by this notification or part there of may ,within 30 days from the date of
publication in the official gazette, file and appeal before Superintending Engineer, Sindhudurg Irrigation
Circle, Sindhudurg within 30 days from the date of publication of this notification in the Government
Gazette.

BY THE ADDITIONAL DIRECTOR GENERAL OF POLICE (TRAFFIC)
MAHARASHTRA STATE, MUMBAI
Read: (1) Section 112 of Motor Vehicle Act, 1988.
(2) Notification No. MVA 0589/CR 1061/TRA-2, dated 19th May 1990 And Addendum
dated 30th September 2002, published in Maharashtra Government Gazette, Part IV-,
(Extraordinary) on 30th September 2002.
(3) Ministry of Road Transport & Highways notification No.1377, dated 6th April 2018.
(4) Notification No. ADG (Tr.)/45/Planning/3105/2019, dated 25th October 2019 Additional
Director General of Police (Traffic), State of Maharashtra, Mumbai.
(5) MSRDC letter No. ow 2026/2026, dated 27th April 2026.
(6) SP HSP Raigad letter No. ow 407/2026, dated 22th April 2026.
(7) SP HSP Pune letter No. ow 531/2026, dated 16th April 2026.
(8) Notification No. ADG (Tr.)/45/Planning/1482/2026, Dated 27th April 2026 Additional
Director General of Police (Traffic), State of Maharashtra, Mumbai.
CORRIGENDUM
No.ADGP(T)/45/Planning/1528/2026.___ The “Missing Link” route on the Yashwantrao
Chavan Mumbai-Pune Expressway is being opened for traffic from 1st May 2026. Accordingly, a
notification has been issued by this office vide reference no. 8.
In the said notification, the following content was published for the first phase.
(A) Phased Commencement of Traffic :—
Phase I (From 1st May 2026 to 31st October 2026)
Entry is allowed exclusively to Light Motar Vehicles (LMVs) and Passenger Busses/
Passenger Vehicles.
Goods carrying vehicles are prohibited.
However, partial amendments are being made in the said content, and the following
corrigendum is being issued.
(A) Phased Commencement of Traffic :—
Phase I (From 1st May 2026 to 31st October 2026)
Only non-transport Light Motor Vehicles (LMV) and passenger Vehicles (including buses)
are permitted on the Missing Link.
All goods carrying Vehicles, including LMV goods vehicles (such as Pickup vans, Tata Ace,
etc.) are strictly prohibited.
Mumbai, PRAVEEN SALUNKE,
dated 30th April 2026. Additional Director General of Police (Traffic),
Maharashtra State, Mumbai.
